## 10. Decision record · open questions

### 10-1. Closed user decisions (2026-09-25 · 2026-09-26 — not reopened)

The source of truth for the wording is PRD §2·§3, and the record of answers is PRD §9. Below, one line each states the answers this document leans on together with the landing place, and where a decision reversed an edition 1 recommendation or an earlier decision, the user's reason is left.

1. **Delivery = 「에이전트가 받을 수 있는 가장 이른 때」** (PRD §3-1) — claude as soon as received to stdin · codex holds while holding is not too late and hands over at once at the boundary · in segments where holding makes it late, at once. The user changed an earlier decision (「두 백엔드 공통 받자마자 넘긴다」 that closed edition 1's Q1 as (c) — user: 「일단은 이렇게 공용화하고 나중에 세분화」) into a principle: codex entered the same boundary even when held until tool end (M7 10/10 — §10-2), and user: 「이것은 나중 일이 아니라 이 기능의 일부다」. The landing order (skeleton → measurement → holding) was decided by this document. → §4-2 · §5-5 · §9
2. **✕ = always on every item of the list — both backends · in any segment · pressing it removes from the list** (PRD §3-6 · user decision 2026-09-25 — 「x를 건드릴 이유가 있어? 그냥 x누르면 목록에서 기계적으로 빼는거잖아. 구지 비활성 필요 있냐고」 · for a handed-over item received late and standing as a bubble, 「상관없어」). If it can be withdrawn the message is also withdrawn — claude always for a list item (vendor cancel request) · codex while we hold it (path verb `withdraw` — no vendor call) · an already handed-over codex item is only removed from the list. The LLM also cancels any item of the list with the same command (PRD AC18 — the verb-own rejection is only `NOT_FOUND` · a lease rejection is the common entrance's, §5-6). The codex connecting ✕ (Q2) is the ✕ of a held item so it withdraws the message from the skeleton (P2) on (§5-5). The design that turned ✕ on and off per segment (`cancellable`) was withdrawn (§10-5). The outcome is by the lifecycle — `cancelled:false` is not 「전달됨」 (only the response `true` is cancelled at once — it is the vendor's answer that it removed it, §5-2). → §5-4 · §5-5 · §5-6
3. **✕ hides at once in every window · 「취소 중」 only in the LLM list** (Q7 · Q7 follow-up — user: 「x 누르면 바로 빠지는 거잖아. 다른 창도 바로 빠져야 되는 거 아니?」). If late it just appears as a bubble in every window and we do not notify · Tab + tooltip 「목록에서 빼기」 (2026-09-26 — 21 below) · no guidance line (also when ✕ could not withdraw the message — the bubble of outcome (b) is all). → §5-2 · §5-6 · §5-7
4. **A withdrawn message·one that could not be sent is discarded — no return to the input box · no notification** (Q1 · PRD §3-7). It reversed edition 1's Q2 recommendation (return to the input box) — user: 「일단 그냥 다 버리고 나중에 생각하자」. What could not be sent through interrupt·agent end·rejection is also discarded (confirmed the night of 2026-09-25). If a bubble drawn while idle is rejected it is erased without a notification (Q1 ④ — 「거절하면 우리쪽 채팅도 지워져야」) — we apply the same principle also to the rejection after receipt of a list item (§5-2). If a bubble drawn while idle meets an interrupt·agent end before being processed, it stays (N9 — 「그냥 별도로 뭐 해야되는게 있음? resume 하면 어차피 갱신될텐데」). → §5-2 · §5-7
5. **There is no header line above the list** (Q4 · N2 — 「한 줄 낭비」). It reversed the header line (「준비되면 전달」 · 「도구 호출이 끝나면 전달」) that edition 1 decided in Q3 (a). → §5-7
6. **The collapsed list** = press 「외 N개」 to expand · while collapsed the three oldest · full text in tooltip · count·shape to be adjusted later (Q3). → §5-7
7. **The turn-interrupt operation = the very next feature after this one** (Q5). → §1-2 · §5-4 「끊기」
8. **A message sent while resuming** (Q6) — in codex, if the conversation continued but only the on-screen history restore failed, it is handed over as it is · for claude, if it is loading, it was list + hand over at once, but ★we have no loading signal on our side so we leave today's behavior (a bubble at once) — the claude part of PRD §3-11 · AC28 is an exception (N7 (a) — user: 「그냥 내가 채팅 치다가 오면 병합 잘 되는거잖아. 이번에 순서 삐뚤어져도 맞추는거 추가하잖아.」)★. → §5-4 · §5-5
9. **A turn that ended in error** (Q8) — codex does not send on its own and waits, going into one turn with the next user message, the remaining first · even if the limit lifts it does not go on its own (the user wants us not to touch the session) · claude as the vendor does (「억지로 구현하지 마」) · a message with no answer that already became a bubble is left as it is. On 2026-09-26 this answer widened into the 「오류 뒤 멈춤」 that also blocks mail on both backends (18 below). → §5-4 · §5-5
10. **Several left at turn end** (Q9) = together in one next turn, each separately (codex · claude is the vendor — M11). → §5-5
11. **User input before mail** (N6) — mail's idle = no turn running ∧ user list empty · mail may be delayed (including the halt after error — 18 below) · terminal-mode mail is as today. The 「오늘 시점과의 편차」 that N6 had asked about was replaced by this rule (PRD AC22 changed). → §5-0 · §5-5
12. **Even if a user item whose receipt never comes blocks mail, we put no ceiling** (N8) — user: 「사용자 글이 막히면 우편도 막혀야 하는 거 아냐? 애초에 글이 막히는 것 자체가 이상한 건데」. → §5-0 · §8
13. **Old vendor versions = today's behavior**, no special support (N1 (a) — 「알아서 업데이트」). → §4-3 · §5-4 · §5-5
14. **`PROTOCOL_VERSION` 5 → 6** (N4 approved). → §8
15. **The client matches live delivery order — the core invariant as it is** (N5 (a) — agreed after seeing a concrete example: the pump thread's `Delivered{X}` and the input thread's `Queued{Y}` arrive with seq reversed). → §5-7
16. **What is unchanged** — display = the list above the input box · scope = JSON mode only · mail is outside the list · storage = the daemon's agent session layer · receipt = the vendor's per-item notification · idle = no turn ∧ list empty · cancel = a registered command (Esc later) · messages stay separate · pulling in by hook (S4) rejected.
17. **An item handed over in codex whose acceptance is never known is kept in the list until the agent ends** (N10 — user: 「갑자기 목록이 사라져서 이상할것같은데 … 목록에서 뺀다는건 이유가 있어야되는거아냐」). Leaving the list happens only when there is a reason (receipt · ✕ · agent end). It reversed the discard (revival if the echo is late) that the edition 7 draft had written. → §5-5 「수락 모름」 · §7-3 AC9
18. **N11 → widen (b) into a principle — 「오류 뒤 멈춤」** (2026-09-26 — user: 「진짜 에러면 강제로 쑤셔넣으면 안되잖아」 · 「어쨋든 본질은 가능하면 자동으로 되고, 거기에 유저게 우선한다는 얘기지」 · 「어쨋든 모종의 이유로 이상하게 종료될때 채팅 입력이 성공하거나 어쨋든 재개 신호가 오면 뿌린다는거잖아」 → 「ㅇㅇ」). Not issuing an owed empty turn again if it fails — beyond that, after a turn that ended oddly through a real error (request limit · overload · context exceeded) or a request-stage failure, neither user messages nor mail are put in on their own, and things flow again only when the user's next input turn ends in success (if that turn also fails it keeps halting). Mail is on both backends — we are the ones who put mail in so it is independent of vendor behavior · claude list items as the vendor does (9) · the mail halt is on all versions (round-6 review R1 — a delegated judgment, not a user answer) (for old codex below the floor the path behaves as today, so only the automatic-turn halt `halted` is absent — 13) · `interrupted` and steer write failure are not triggers (an interrupt does not release either — both backends) · the alternative of continuing on its own at a limit-release signal was rejected (「아냐 일단 복잡하게 하지말자」 · §10-5) · accepted cost = with the list empty, mail halts on screen for no visible reason (a mail notification is backlog outside this feature). The place that blocks (mail = on both backends `TurnFact.last_end_failed` of the turn table → kernel `BusyPolicy` · turns codex opens by itself = path field `halted`) was chosen by this document (decision — round-6 review moved the door of codex mail from the path to a kernel fact, §10-5). → §5-0 · §5-4 · §5-5 「오류 뒤 멈춤」 · §5-9 · §10-4 N11
19. **N12 → (a) the ✕ of an acceptance-unknown codex item closes at once** (2026-09-26 — user: 「끝난걸로쳐. 유저가 x눌렀을때 우연찮게 들어가는건 그냥 그렇다 치겠지」). At the moment of ✕ it closes as discard (`Dropped{Unknown}`), leaves the LLM list too, and does not stay in 「취소 중」 (the three events are chained at once) · if nothing else blocks, mail flows · if a late echo comes it is an end-of-conversation bubble (we accepted that an outcome written as discard flips to a bubble — if it is an echo after the turn end, no turn to answer is opened on its own, 22). It is written as an exception in PRD AC11 · AC13. → §5-1 · §5-2 · §5-5 · §7-3
20. **N13 → (b) a message typed after an acceptance-unknown item is handed over in the order typed as usual** (2026-09-26 — user: 「음? 뭔소리야 순서대로 들어가는거잖아. 앞지른다는게 뭔소리? 또 발생되지도 않는걸 가정함?」). That item is a message already handed over to codex, so a later message going after it is the order typed (C → D → E · the no-overtaking ban is among `Held`). That item stays gray + ✕ and what it blocks is mail alone (N8 — it is visible in the list so the reason it is blocked is on screen). If the echo is late, that bubble may stand after the bubbles of the messages handed over in between — accepted since it is a screen position inside a defensive branch never observed (PRD §3-4 · AC2 exception). → §5-1 · §5-5 hand-over rule · Idle order · §5-6 · §7-1
21. **✕ tooltip = 「목록에서 빼기」** (2026-09-26 — all items · the same wording on both backends · no warning attached. User: 「계속 몇번을 얘기하는데 x눌러도 없어졌는데 안으로 들어가서 표시하는건 어쩔수 없잖아.」 · earlier on 2026-09-25 「x를 건드릴 이유가 있어? 그냥 x누르면 목록에서 기계적으로 빼는거잖아」). It replaces the old wording 「취소 — 글은 버려짐」 of 3 (a wrong statement for a handed-over codex item). → §5-7
22. **Design stance — give up a little on rare branches and keep it simple** (2026-09-26 — user: 「복잡한것보다 약간 양보하면서 단순한 방향으로 하되 나중에 문제되면 기능 추가하면 되지」 · 「일단 유저 채팅입력 관해서는 너무 깐깐하게 안해도 된다는거지」). For rare defensive branches (acceptance unknown · the end-of-turn 「기록만」 · late echo) we choose leaving them as they are + a warning log instead of adding states·rules and write them as 「나중에 더할 후보」 · old rules that the new halt covers are deleted. With this stance we withdrew the end-of-turn probe · the hidden record after ✕ · the debt of a late echo after the turn end (a late echo only stands a bubble and the next user turn sees it from the history). → §5-5 · §10-5 「나중에 더할 후보」
23. **The orchestrator takes the TRD finalization and the implementation and gives a final report at the end** (2026-09-26 — user: 「나 자러갈테니깐 쭉쭉 진행해. 충분히 얘기한것같으니 구현 너가 알아서하고 후에 최종 보고하는걸로」). Order = head 「다음 순서」 · §9. If a new divergence in user-visible feel arises during implementation, it is also raised in §10-4 at that time.
24. **The session id of codex JSON is persisted when the peer first echoes a user message back** (edition 9 · during implementation 2026-09-26 — user: 「세션 id가 저장되는건 우리가 보내는 시점이 아니고 오는 시점이잖아」 → 「제출하는게 아니고 코덱스가 뱉는거 기준으로 저장하라고 최초 뱉을때가 완전한 타이밍인거잖아. 미세하게 중간에 종료되서 저장안되는건 그냥 초기화 하라고하고」 → (asked: A = first echo-back · B = the `thread/start` response) → 「일단 실제 아이디가 생성된 기준이어야지 당연히. 그게 사용자 글을 에코할때잖아.」). If it dies after sending and before the echo-back, nothing is persisted and the next activation is a new conversation. Scope = codex JSON only (claude · codex terminal as they are). We amend ADR-0226 decisions 2 · 11 with a new ADR. → §5-8 implementation reflected · §10-6

### 10-2. Record of a measurement overturning one ground of a user decision — closed

- **What:** when codex was handed over at the tool **end** (S1) it entered the same boundary **10/10** — a window of 6–64 ms from the tool completion notification to the check of waiting items (M7 · §3-2). The ground on which the user rejected S1 (「한 걸음 늦다」) held only for claude (0/10).
- **Closed:** the user knew that measurement, set the principle 「받을 수 있는 가장 이른 때 전달」, and decided to hold codex until the earliest boundary (2026-09-25 — §10-1 1). So S1 is rejected only for claude and adopted for codex (§10-5).
- **The two measurements that were the premise are green** — our daemon's reaction delay (M15) and the window at the end of an answer with no tools (M16) → P8 landed (§3-3 · §9 P7 — the stop condition for when they were red and its record are also in §9 P7).
- It is carried into the decision history of the new ADR (§8).

### 10-3. PRD §9 questions — all answered

All of PRD §9's Q1–Q9 received answers on 2026-09-25. The answers and the landing places are collected in §10-1, and the implementation pieces where those answers land are 「사용자 결정이 착지하는 조각」 below the §9 table. The table that listed branches per answer was withdrawn because the answers are settled (discarded branches = §10-5).

### 10-4. Questions this document raised

For closed ones only one answer line is kept (collection = §10-1). ★**There are no open questions**★ — N1–N13 are all closed (N3 does not arise because M9 is green · N11 · N12 · N13 received answers on 2026-09-26). No wait-for-user-confirmation marker remains in the body.

- **N1 old vendor versions — closed:** (a) today's behavior as it is (user: 「알아서 업데이트」). The (b) branch that put up the list but supported it without ✕ was withdrawn (§10-5). → §4-3 · §5-3 · §5-4 · §5-5
- **N2 the header line of 「턴 없음 + 목록 있음」 — closed:** with Q4 the header line went away so the field itself does not exist.
- **N3 when there is no means to open a turn to answer a codex item that got no answer — does not arise (M9 green):** an empty `turn/start` (`input: []`) was accepted 4/4 and answered only the message that got no answer (Phase 0b — `docs/research/mid-turn-phase0b-measurements-2026-09-26.md` · §3-3). The control group (when there was nothing without an answer) repeated the previous answer — the debt is set only when it really got no answer (§5-5). The road of filling by resending is still not an option (PRD §3-1 · §3-7).
- **N4 `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 5 → 6 — closed:** we raise it (user approval). → §8
- **N5 live delivery order — closed:** (a) the client matches by seq continuity and the core invariant (ADR-0006 「lock 미보유 send」) is as it is (user confirmation). (b), which changes the core, is §10-5. → §5-7
- **N6 mail timing·order — closed:** beyond whether to allow deviations, **user input before mail** became the rule — mail's idle = no turn running ∧ user list empty (PRD §3-9 · AC22). The three deviations that were asked about were absorbed into that rule — mail waiting for settlement (①) and mail pushed behind the drain turn (②) are because the user list is not empty during that time, and that a user message that came after held mail gets its answer first (③) is now a rule and not a deviation. → §5-0 · §5-5
- **N7 a message sent while claude is loading a resumed conversation — closed:** (a) today's behavior as it is — a bubble at once and that short window has no list·✕ (the claude part of PRD §3-11 · AC28 is an exception). User: 「그냥 내가 채팅 치다가 오면 병합 잘 되는거잖아. 이번에 순서 삐뚤어져도 맞추는거 추가하잖아.」 · 「애초에 코덱스쪽에서 리스트를 먼저 보내주겠지 시작하자마자」. (b), which always stands the first user input of a resuming incarnation in the list, is §10-5. → §5-4
- **N8 may a user item whose receipt never comes block mail forever — closed:** we put no ceiling — a blocked user item also blocks mail (user: 「사용자 글이 막히면 우편도 막혀야 하는 거 아냐? 애초에 글이 막히는 것 자체가 이상한 건데」). A blocked item is an anomaly (a missed signal), and an intended long wait (the halt after error — both backends · §10-1 18) is already blocking mail by that rule. It is an intended exception to ADR-0104 「늦게 가는 것 < 안 가는 것」 (§8) · the 30-minute ceiling is §10-5. → §5-0
- **N9 if a bubble drawn while idle meets an interrupt·agent end before getting an answer (not a rejection) — closed:** we leave it (today's behavior · §5-2 「거절만 지운다」). User: 「그냥 별도로 뭐 해야되는게 있음? resume 하면 어차피 갱신될텐데」. We did not check whether the bubble of a message the vendor did not record drops out on a resume reload. → §5-2
- **N10 an item handed over in codex whose receipt by the vendor is never known — closed:** (b) keep it in the list until the agent ends (the PRD wording as it is — PRD §3-4 · AC9). User: 「갑자기 목록이 사라져서 이상할것같은데 … 목록에서 뺀다는건 이유가 있어야되는거아냐」 — leaving the list happens only when there is a reason (receipt = a bubble · ✕ · agent end). The targets are five places — the exits where the `turn/start` response does not come within the deadline (30 s), cannot be decoded, or the turn id exceeds the ceiling · the end-of-turn settlement deadline (the steer response does not come) · an item with a success response but no echo at turn end (a branch that should not exist per the source — M10 green · the probe was withdrawn in edition 8) (§5-5 「수락 모름」). It is a rare branch when the vendor does not answer for over 30 s.
  - **✕ and a late echo** — ✕ closes at once as discard per N12 (a) (below). If a late echo comes it is a receipt as usual (an end-of-conversation bubble), and if it is an echo after the turn ended, no turn to answer is opened on its own (an edition 8 concession — the next user turn sees it from the history · §10-5 「나중에 더할 후보」). There is no resend (PRD §3-7 — double delivery). The acceptance unknown of a message sent while idle (Direct) has no event (the bubble already stands and it never went on the list).
  - **Accepted cost** — while that item stands, mail cannot go in (no ceiling on `inputs_pending` — N8 · it is visible in the list so the reason it is blocked is on screen) · meanwhile new messages pass through the list (loss of AC1 — the same kind as PRD §3-4 「받음이 끝내 오지 않으면」) · if it was not received, that message never goes and stays gray (it can be removed with ✕). It does not block later messages (N13 (b)). The branch edition 7 worried about, where a 「기록만」 item stays gray, disappeared because M10 showed that the echo comes before the turn end. The rejected (a) (discard, and revive if the echo is late) is §10-5. → §5-5 「수락 모름」 · §7-3 AC9
- **N11 if the owed follow-up turn (empty turn) ends in failure — closed (2026-09-26):** (b) widened into a principle — after an oddly ended turn (codex = `turn/completed(failed)` · the five exits of `turn/start` — all of user · Direct · empty · mail turns / claude = error `result`) neither automatic turns nor mail are put in, and it is released only when the user's next input turn ends in success (「오류 뒤 멈춤」 · mail is on both backends · all versions (round-6 review R1 — a delegated judgment, not a user answer) — codex below the floor lacks only the automatic-turn halt). The empty turn is not issued again — the debt is released the moment it is issued, and that message is already in the history so the next user turn sees it together. User: 「진짜 에러면 강제로 쑤셔넣으면 안되잖아」 (all quotations = §10-1 18). Practice ground = [`docs/research/queued-input-after-failed-turn-2026-09-26.md`](../../research/queued-input-after-failed-turn-2026-09-26.md) (codex automatically submitting queued input after a failure was reported as a defect — openai/codex#24443). The rejected (a) (give up and let mail flow at once) and automatic resumption at a limit-release signal are §10-5. → §5-0 · §5-4 · §5-5 「오류 뒤 멈춤」 · §5-9 · §9
- **N12 a ✕ pressed on an acceptance-unknown item in codex — closed (2026-09-26):** (a) closes at once — at the moment of ✕ it closes as discard (`Dropped{Unknown}` — outcome (c)), leaves the LLM list too, and does not stay in 「취소 중」 (it chains `CancelRequested` → `CancelAnswered{removed:false}` → `Dropped{Unknown}` at once) · when the registry empties mail flows. If the echo is late it is revived as an end-of-conversation bubble (we accepted that an outcome written as discard flips to a bubble · for an echo after the turn end no turn to answer is opened on its own — §10-1 22). User: 「끝난걸로쳐. 유저가 x눌렀을때 우연찮게 들어가는건 그냥 그렇다 치겠지」. PRD AC11 · AC13 wrote the exception. The rejected (b) (「취소 중」 until receipt or agent end) is §10-5. → §5-1 · §5-2 · §5-5 「✕ 한 항목의 수락 모름」 · §7-3
- **N13 a message typed after an acceptance-unknown item in codex — closed (2026-09-26):** (b) handed over in the order typed as usual — that item is a message already handed over, so a later message going after it is the order typed (C → D → E · the no-overtaking ban is among `Held`). That item stays gray + ✕ and what it blocks is mail alone (N8). If the echo is late, that bubble may stand after the bubbles of the messages handed over in between (accepted — PRD §3-4 · AC2 exception). User: 「음? 뭔소리야 순서대로 들어가는거잖아. 앞지른다는게 뭔소리? 또 발생되지도 않는걸 가정함?」 The rejected (a) (hold it behind that item) is §10-5. → §5-5 hand-over rule · Idle order · §5-6 · §7-1

### 10-5. ADR candidates — rejected alternatives and reasons (carried into the new ADR 「거부한 대안」)

| Rejected alternative | Reason | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Fix a regular bubble at the sent place (today's claude · ADR-0198 original · the survey's B) | If it is mid-turn, the rest of the earlier answer continues below it and the order gets mixed | user · PRD §6 |
| A gray waiting row at the bottom of the conversation (the survey report's recommended A) | 「터미널이 가장 자연스럽다」 | user |
| Hold until the turn ends (today's codex) | 「당연히 도구 호출 끝나면 바로 푸쉬」 | user |
| Hold claude and hand over after seeing tool **completion** (S1 — ★rejected only for claude · adopted for codex★) | One step late — the agent first snapshots its own waiting box at the tool end, so a message handed over after that is read only when the next tool ends (user confirmation · M7 claude 0/10). In codex the same method entered the same boundary (M7 10/10), so we adopted it as the tool segment of 「가장 이른 경계까지 붙듦」 (§4-2 · §5-5 · §10-2) | user · M7 · PRD §6 |
| Pull in by hook (S4) | 「훅마다 매번 뭔가 실행하면 안 된다」 · the message it puts in is model context and not a user turn · codex hook trust is a road ADR-0216 withdrew | user · §3-1 · ADR-0216 |
| Hold and hand over at tool **start** (S5 · edition 1's codex recommendation) | Even if held until the tool *ends*, the delivery timing is the same and the cancel window is longer — the principle 「붙들어도 늦지 않는 동안 붙든다」 chooses the tool-end side. The old reason 「공용화가 먼저, 세분화는 나중」 was replaced by that principle | user · PRD §6 |
| Join the waiting items together at once | Messages stay separate (PRD §3-8) — if joined, the per-item ids merge into one and per-item receipt·✕·bubbles cannot stand · each backend needs a workaround. (「받자마자 넘기면 모아 둘 대기분이 없다」 fits only claude — codex holds until the boundary) | user · PRD §6 · survey §5 |
| Return a withdrawn message·one that could not be sent to the input box (edition 1 Q2 recommendation) | 「일단 그냥 다 버리고 나중에 생각하자」 — deferred (PRD §7) | user |
| WS/net-layer storage · frontend (per window) storage | Authority splits because each connection·window is separate and it disappears on reconnect · net was built not to know agents (ADR-0129) | §4-1 · user confirmed |
| codex `thread/queue/*` | An experimental API · it drains when the thread becomes idle so tool-boundary delivery does not happen · holding is already done by our path | §3-2 · §4-3 |
| Read claude `cancelled:false` as 「전달됨」 | On the boundary `started` comes after `false`, and a reservation cancel also answers `false` — the outcome by the lifecycle (the exception = the one response `true` — cancelled at once, §5-2 · edition 4: `false`·error responses are used only for the cause attribution of `cancelled`) | §3-1 · M3 |
| Treat the `cancelled` of a cancel-pending item unconditionally as 「취소됨」 (edition 3) | `cancelled` also comes when a failed turn closes the waiting items — what is not a user cancel would look like a cancel → we tell the cause apart by checking against our cancel response (what the vendor description requires) | §5-2 · §3-1 |
| Load several items into one `turn/start` | They merge into one `userMessage`·one `clientId` so messages do not stay separate (PRD §3-8) | §5-5 |
| 「`turn/start` 를 쓴 순간 = 받음」 on codex below the floor (edition 1 「고름」) | What was written in is not a receipt (PRD §3-4) → lowered to an N1 option, and the user decided old versions get today's behavior (N1 (a)) | PRD §3-4 · user (N1) |
| Pairing bubbles by content comparison | ADR-0198 already rejected it — both backends have item ids | ADR-0198 |
| Carrying the waiting event in the `Structured` escape hatch | It is a field for per-backend originals and the claude turn classifier counts it as a progress signal | §5-2 |
| Carrying the list in SubscribeAck | The shell marker (fixed length) would have to change → replaced by re-attach reconciliation | §5-7 |
| Writing progress (Progress) to the turn table in the Queued branch (edition 3 review · reason corrected in edition 4) | The input path would become a third kind of writer to the turn table (outside ADR-0127's causal argument). The other two reasons of edition 3 (false progress · no harm to mail) were wrong — what we wanted to get (seeing the drain turn early · closing the mail-fold gap) is obtained by **counting the claude `Delivered`, a translation of a vendor line, as progress** | §5-4 · ADR-0127 |
| A separate classification-only 「벤더 턴 신호」 (edition 3) | If claude `Delivered` (=`started`) is counted as progress, the core turn observation gives the same value at the same moment — a second overlapping table · the mail busy decision could not see that signal so a gap remained | §5-4 |
| Putting the cannot-receive decision transition inside the input lock | The transition arises in the pump and that lock contains the latch commit (disk write) — if the pump waited, output would stand behind disk I/O → replaced by rewriting inside the core replay lock (edition 5 reason correction — edition 4's 「stdin 쓰기와 순환 대기」 was wrong: `send_input` only puts into a queue) | §5-3 · §5-4 · §5-0 |
| Branching in the session by encoder tag·backend name (edition 2) | Backend knowledge leaks into the session → replaced by the mid-turn input policy·receipt-possibility the backend reports | §5-0 · ADR-0004 |
| A 「가장 최근」 default if there is no `input_id` (edition 2) | It is a default for an Esc that does not exist yet — reopen it when Esc is brought in | §5-6 |
| Keep `PROTOCOL_VERSION` (edition 2) | The new command is answered with `Error{request_id: None}` by an old daemon and the new shell hangs until the deadline (v3 precedent) | §8 |
| Probe also items before the steer response at turn end (edition 2) | ★Edition 8 — the end-of-turn probe was withdrawn (「턴 끝 탐침」 row below) so there is no dispute in this row★ · old reason: if the response is −32600 it overlaps the probe 「없음」 and `turn/start` goes out twice → wait for the response and tell by generation | §5-5 |
| A follow-up turn mark per item (edition 3 — per item together with the probe) | If several items in one turn have no answer, several follow-up turns would open and answers overlap → one settlement per turn · the follow-up turn debt is one per turn (the probe was withdrawn in edition 8 — this reason remains only for the debt) | §5-5 |
| Reading the 「없음」 of one probe page (`limit`·`desc`) as absence (edition 3) | ★Edition 8 — the end-of-turn probe was withdrawn (「턴 끝 탐침」 row below) so there is no dispute in this row★ · old reason: in a long turn that item may be outside the first page → a message already in the history is sent again (double delivery) → walk until T is passed entirely, and if truncated, unknown (acceptance unknown — it stays in the list, N10) | §5-5 · §3-2 |
| Narrowing the probe to `turnId` | ★Edition 8 — the end-of-turn probe was withdrawn (「턴 끝 탐침」 row below) so there is no dispute in this row★ · old reason: we do not know under which turn id a 「기록만」 item is carried (M10 ④) — if it drops out, 「없음」 misjudges → double delivery | §5-5 |
| Releasing the follow-up turn mark by 「`Delivered` 뒤에 열린 턴」 (edition 3) | ★Edition 8 — a late echo after the turn end sets no debt (candidate to add later) · 「내는 순간 푼다」 as it is★ · a user turn already opened before the late echo saw that message and yet one more follow-up turn would open → 「그 턴이 끝난 뒤 낸 사용자 턴·빈 턴」 releases it (the moment it is issued · a mail turn does not release — row below) | §5-5 |
| A mail turn also releases the follow-up turn debt · Idle order = user `Held` → mail → empty turn (edition 7 draft) | The debt arose because a user message got no answer (PRD §3-1 · AC6) — if that message becomes `Delivered` the registry empties and the kernel lets mail go, and if the mail turn opens first the model answers the mail and the user message still gets no answer (PRD §3-9 「사용자 입력이 먼저」) → Idle order = user `Held` → debt (empty turn) → mail · a mail turn does not release the debt | PRD §3-9 · §5-5 |
| Adding the follow-up turn debt to the kernel's busy fact (`inputs_pending`) to block mail | The waiting list table is one set the core writes only inside registry reduction (§5-0 writing order · filtering late writes by seq · the doorbell at the moment of emptying). The debt is the path's turn field, so adding it would give the table a second writing side and add one more doorbell at the moment the debt is released → we block it by the path's Idle order (mail the kernel released earlier is held in the path and takes that order) | §5-5 · §5-0 |
| Suppressing the bubble of a late echo after discarding a ✕-ed item as acceptance unknown · keeping the bubble and 「버려짐」 together | The former = the message the model received disappears from the screen · the latter = one message has two outcomes → revival (the echo beats unknown) | §5-5 · §5-2 |
| Not putting a deadline branch in the steer·probe waiting table (edition 3) | A steer with no response stays in `InFlight{Pending}` forever and settlement and the whole codex queue stop → expiry = acceptance unknown (edition 8 — it stays in the list with no probe, N10) | §5-5 |
| Putting the bus lease check in `DaemonLocalCommands::run` | The CLI `/control/agent` does not pass that function and calls the common entrance directly — the CLI would drop out of the check → we put it in `call_daemon_command`, which both surfaces pass together | §5-6 |
| Putting the loading gate change in the screen step (P5) (edition 3) | If it stands first without a producer, the loading sheet stays longer than today on a resumed codex with no history → together with the producer (P2) | §9 |
| Blocking live delivery reversal by gathering list events into one producer (edition 5 review) | The line stands only among list events, and the reversal where a pump conversation frame overtakes and makes a list event dropped stays | §5-7 |
| When we see a hole, reconcile only the list with `listQueuedInputs` (edition 5 review) | Even if the list comes back, the bubble of the lost `Delivered` (the echo-back is already suppressed) and the conversation lines cannot come back → client seq continuity (the alternative that changes the invariant is N5) | §5-7 |
| Re-attach reconciliation = the union since subscription **minus ids that saw a terminal** (edition 7 draft) | A `CancelRequested` that came before the answer after the query read `Queued` is not a terminal but a late row redrawing an item that was ✕-ed (PRD §3-6 「다시 나타나지 않는다」) → the answer carries the snapshot seq and re-reduces the events after it over the row. We also dropped 「대조 창에 사건을 본 id 는 행을 버린다」 — for an item whose `Queued` was pushed off the ring, dropping the row loses the body copy and the bubble of outcome (b) cannot be drawn | §5-7 |
| A separate deadline · page ceiling for each side of settlement's response·probe (edition 4) | ★Edition 8 — there is no probe so the settlement deadline is put only on waiting for the response★ · old reason: if continued it is about 60 s and a multi-page probe has no end · with two ceilings we measure the same thing twice → one settlement deadline measured from the turn end | §5-5 |
| Telling steer response `Accepted`·`Expired` apart (edition 4) | If both have no echo at turn end, they get the same disposition (edition 8 — acceptance unknown · no probe) so there is nowhere to tell them apart → one value 「응답 대기 중인가」 | §5-5 |
| Probe-settling a `turn/start` that ended with acceptance unknown the way a turn end is (review decision original) | ★Edition 8 — the end-of-turn probe was withdrawn (「턴 끝 탐침」 row below) so there is no dispute in this row★ · old reason: those exits have no T turn id so the probe's stop condition does not stand (walking would just reach the deadline and be unknown), and if it was received the echo comes at the turn start and that echo gives the same outcome → acceptance unknown with no probe (it stays in the list — N10) | §5-5 |
| Reverting an acceptance-unknown `turn/start` to `Held` and sending again | Unlike steer it is not tied to a turn, so if the server processes it late it is a double delivery | §5-5 |
| Limiting revival to 「`Dropped{Unknown}` 사건이 만든 묘비」 (a place in edition 4) | The cause is the same yet it splits by closing event, and the definitions at two places in the document diverged → one set by cause `Unknown` | §5-2 |
| Emitting the termination synthesis singly per id (edition 4) | Each call takes the lock separately so a `Queued` that stands between the sweep and the seal slips past both → one unit | §5-3 |
| Blocking N1 (a)'s write failure with a 「썼다」 confirmation event or a transition that reopens the synthetic-receipt tombstone | Vocabulary·transitions grow and the drawn bubble has to be erased → only the `Unknown` branch emits `Queued` after the write | §5-4 |
| Leaving out the input lock (edition 2 — 「취소가 글을 앞지르는 경합은 자연히 안 생긴다」) | `Queued` is visible before the write so another window·the LLM can press first — if the cancel line reaches before the message line, no reservation is set mid-turn and the message is delivered while the screen trusts the cancel (M3 c2) → session input lock | §5-0 · §3-1 |
| Deciding the reduction rule by history (「앞에 `CancelRequested` 가 있었나」) (edition 2) | The golden sequence would lean on history search and not stand deterministically → a table based on present state | §5-2 |
| Accepting the idle misclassification in the drain turn gap (edition 2) | A bubble in the sent place arises on every long turn so PRD AC3 breaks systematically, and in the same gap mail is folded into the user's drain turn → count claude `Delivered` as progress | §5-4 |
| Landing the producers (P2·P3) before the screen (P5) (edition 2) | Mid-turn input emits only `Queued` with no synthetic echo and there is no list to draw so nothing shows until received — worse than today → P5 first | §9 |
| Skipping the hole in live sorting after a short deadline (edition 5) | It is a magic number that decides the answer (ADR-0038) · the overflow disposition's re-request does not run in live (`ladderRerequest` re-requests only in buffering — `protocolClient.ts:418-437`) → a placeholder so the sink does not skip seq · the hold is bounded only by the view buffer ceiling · if it overflows, a full replay with `startBuffering` | §5-7 |
| Flushing the buffer with only sorting and skipping holes (through edition 5 · today's code) | `ReplayComplete` goes into the connection queue outside the subscribers lock so reversed live frames split before and after the marker — if frames after the hole are flushed first, live dedup drops the late hole frame → only as far as connected from `max(마지막+1, replay_from)` | §5-7 |
| Estimating the replay head of the flush start point as the buffer minimum seq (edition 6 draft) | Wrong when replay is empty — in a new agent, if seq 1 is before the marker and 0 after, it flushes from 1 and drops 0 → the shell carries the `replay_from` of SubscribeAck in the marker (shell↔webview only · wire unchanged) | §5-7 |
| Returning the sink discard branches as `SinkError` | The core removes that sink as dead and with the connection alive only that agent's output silently cuts off → a placeholder `Error` at that seq | §5-7 |
| Taking the turn id from `turn/started` (a place in edition 5) | That notification has no identifier we issued so it cannot be attributed — if an earlier turn's late notification writes an id into the new turn field it never recovers (`transport.rs:322` 「되살리지 말 것」) → the `turn/start` response alone | §5-5 |
| Removing a late-arriving `Queued` from the accumulator list **state** (「이미 그린 uuid」 as a reduction rule) | The reducer does not know bubbles — changing only the accumulator state would diverge from the registry·golden sequence → filter only in drawing | §5-7 |
| Reverting `Queued` to before the write in the `Unknown` branch too to prevent the echo-back arriving first | The race where a message that could not be written becomes a bubble under N1 (a) returns → accept the early arrival and draw one bubble only | §5-4 · §5-7 |
| codex also hands over as soon as received (immediate hand-over common to both backends — once this document's target design) | The user principle 「받을 수 있는 가장 이른 때 전달」 — hold while holding is not too late. codex entered the same boundary even when held until the tool end (M7 10/10). User: this is not a later matter but part of this feature. The policy that hands over at once (`Immediate`) remains only as the first step of landing and as the value for segments where measurement is red | user · M7 · §4-2 · §5-5 |
| Hold codex until the boundary in every segment inside a turn (including right after a boundary · the start of a turn) | A message that came right after a boundary signal or the moment a turn was just opened could miss the coming check of waiting items if held → hold only in the segments where measurement stood 「늦지 않다」 (tool segment · answer segment if M16 is green) · if ambiguous, at once (handing over early is harmless) | §5-5 |
| Count every item that is neither output nor echo in the codex tool segment as a tool — including unknown variants (edition 7 draft · 「넓게 세면 일찍 넘길 뿐」) | The direction was backwards — counting as a tool in `AtEarliestBoundary` is the **holding** side. `plan` is model output (`item/plan/delta`), and `contextCompaction`·`hookPrompt` are not tools, and if an item for which we do not know whether the vendor's check of waiting items comes after it ends is counted as a tool, we would wait for that end signal and miss the check (the late side) — the opposite of this document's 「모호하면 곧바로」 → only the decoder's tool-call vocabulary (6 kinds) is tool · `plan` is an output item (answer segment · its end = the end-of-answer signal · M16 measures it together) · the rest is 「그 밖」 (at once). The reason for dropping the old form that counted only 6 kinds (「목록 밖 항목이 도는 동안 가장 좁은 창에 기댄다」) does not stand because we do not hold items outside the list | §5-5 · §3-2 |
| Per-segment ✕ — hide ✕ after hand-over·at boundary open (a ✕-flag field `cancellable` per item with `true` when held · `false` re-emission when handed over · edition 7) | User: 「x를 건드릴 이유가 있어? 그냥 x누르면 목록에서 기계적으로 빼는거잖아. 구지 비활성 필요 있냐고」 · for a handed-over item received late and standing as a bubble 「상관없어」 → ✕ is always on every item of the list and is 「목록에서 뺀다」 (if it can be withdrawn the message is also withdrawn). The field · re-emission · flicker rule · the command's `CONFLICT` drop out entirely so the surface of the path·reducer·re-attach reconciliation·LLM command shrinks. The three that the edition 7 draft argued over on the same premise also close together — putting a FIFO place in segments where hand-over is impossible and turning ✕ on (it would turn on and off within a few ms) · treating waiting-for-the-turn-id and behind the head of Idle as boundary open (no ✕) · not putting ✕ on codex items in the `Immediate` skeleton. All three were disputes on the premise of tying ✕ to segments (holding while waiting for the turn id as cannot-hand-over itself remains in §5-5 — the writer cannot steer without a turn id) | user (2026-09-25) · §5-5 · §5-6 |
| Keep a codex message sent while idle (Direct) out of `pending` (1:1 with list items — edition 7 draft) | A second input in the same idle moment also becomes Direct, and if the first message opens a turn and then goes out by steer and gets −32600, it returns to `Held` and becomes a held item outside the list — in no window and not in the LLM list yet filling pending, turning every new message into waiting and blocking mail → Direct is also put in `pending` as an out-of-list item (`listed=false`) so the second is Queued · `Queued` is never emitted with a Direct id | §5-5 |
| Judge M15 by the sum of two legs (after the reader receives the completion line) and compare M16's answer segment with that sum (edition 7 draft — the tool-end verdict until before round 3 of the edition 7 review · 「보수적」) | The two legs are measured only after the reader receives that line — the time the completion line waited in the pipe (reader lag) is missing, and not only the end of the answer but the tool end also comes right after a long delta flow (commands·file edits·MCP tools pour output deltas until they end). E.g. if after a test that prints thousands of lines the completion line waits 10 ms in the pipe, even with a two-leg sum of 2 ms the real reaction exceeds the window (6 ms) — holding misses the tool boundary (PRD AC4) yet the stop condition (PRD R8) is not triggered. If we measured the second leg only in the turn-id hop with no delta contention it also comes out small → judge and compare both signals by the **reaction delay**, the sum of ① · reader lag · ② maxima · put commands that pour output and parallel tools into the sample and also measure ② in the hop during a delta flood | §3-3 |
| Emit the vendor's late echo (`userMessage`) as the usual `emit` (edition 7 draft wording — 「벤더 출력이라 오늘처럼」) | An echo after the turn end turns the turn table on again as 「턴 중」 and `TurnEnd` does not come again, so mail is blocked until the 30-minute fail-open (ADR-0127) — today's code does not do it that way either → the path reader's attribution gate as it is (`claims_our_turn`) · settlement·waiting·debt are fields outside `TurnState` | §5-5 |
| Release the follow-up turn debt when a new turn id stands after T (when the generation rises) (edition 7 draft) | If an empty `turn/start` ends by the five exits no turn id stands so the debt remains, and the Idle rule issues an empty `turn/start` again at once and it loops without rest while the vendor rejects → release **the moment** a user·empty `turn/start` is **issued** (regardless of the exit · a failure is a warning log — edition 8: if that turn ends oddly the halt after error stands and that message is seen by the next user turn from the history) | §5-5 |

**Candidates to add later (나중에 더할 후보) — deferral, not rejection** (design stance §10-1 22 · 「나중에 문제되면 기능 추가하면 되지」). The common reason for leaving them out = the simplicity stance + the M9 control group (if an empty turn is opened when there is nothing without an answer, the model repeats the previous answer — even a slightly wrong debt decision puts a duplicate answer in front of the user's eyes · observed once in Phase 0b).

- **The debt of a late echo after the turn end** (idempotent — open one empty turn to answer a message received after the turn ended · the body of editions 2–7): for now only the `Delivered` bubble and a warning log, and the next user turn sees that message from the history (§5-5). It arises only inside a rare branch (acceptance unknown · late echo).
- **The hidden record after ✕** (hold the id of an acceptance-unknown item closed by ✕ outside the list until a user·empty turn is issued after T, and set a debt on a late echo — edition 7 review round 4): without the debt above there is no reason to hold it · that a ✕-ed message happened to go in was accepted by the user (N12 — 「유저가 x눌렀을때 우연찮게 들어가는건 그냥 그렇다 치겠지」). Edition 7 set this against the rejected alternative 「✕ 로 닫은 수락 모름 항목의 id 를 통로가 곧바로 잊는다」, but edition 8 chose that 「곧바로 잊는다」.
- **End-of-turn probe** (table above): if the warning counter really counts 「성공 응답인데 에코 없음」 (in M10 that list carried the item in the head of the `limit:40` page — the ground for when it is revived, §5-5).

### 10-6. Implementation reflected — a collection of what differs from edition 8 (edition 9 · 2026-09-26)

We collect in one place what the implementation (P0–P8) landed differently from the edition 8 statements — each place in the body has 「구현 반영」·「9판」 marks and reasons, and this table is an index. Who decided: **user** = a user decision (§10-1 24) · **orchestrator** = chosen under the delegation by which the user entrusted the TRD finalization and implementation (§10-1 23) — it goes in the final report · **review** = a fix that the implementation review (`/review code deep`) found and the orchestrator accepted under the delegation — it goes in the final report · **plan** = decided before starting by the implementation plan (`plan.md` §6) · **implementation** = landed with the same meaning as the statement in a different shape (no change in feel). The 「8판 서술」 column holds the rejected alternatives for when the new ADR is written (not moved into the §10-5 table — they will surface from here when moved). Original record = `.claude/handoff/attachments/20260926-midturn-impl/contacts.md`.

| Edition 8 statement (8판 서술) (place) | Implementation | Reason | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| The codex JSON session id is persisted when the session counts the submission — 「첫 턴 전에 영속」 (§5-8) | Persisted at the peer's **first live user-message echo-back** (`FirstTurnSink`) · no persistence if it dies before the echo-back (next activation = a new conversation) | The first message withdrawn by ✕ while connecting leaves a zero-turn id · 「실제 아이디가 생성된 기준」 | user |
| Follow-up turn debt = a message handed over is received and then `completed` with no sampling (§5-5 「답 없는 항목」 · settlement 3 · §5-9) | **Only messages carried by `turn/steer`** | A turn `turn/start` opened samples its own input first and 「기록만」 is a steer phenomenon · taken literally an empty `turn/start` would go out in operation → the model repeats the earlier answer (M9 control group) | orchestrator |
| Settlement is when a `InFlight` with no echo exists · pending is not empty during settlement (§5-5) | Settlement = when there is a steer request that did not get a response (`steers_owed` — even after the echo) · it stands with an empty list · Direct requires 「정산 없음」 | If the echo comes before the response the item leaves so settlement does not stand and the next `turn/start` goes out before the response | review (P2 codex FIX) |
| Unrecognizable `turn.status` — no statement (the first landing read it as `completed`) | `TurnClose::Unknown` — disposition = `completed` · halt unchanged · no debt | The kernel folds the same end as 「그 밖」 so if the path released it the halts of the two layers would diverge | review (P2 doc-aware F2) |
| Interrupt = everything held and handed over `Dropped{Interrupted}` (§5-5 · §5-9) | **List items only** — held mail remains and goes out at the next Idle | It diverged from the 「우편」 line (held until the turn end) · discarding would lose the mail | orchestrator (source reading) |
| The error response of `turn/start` — the steer error line and the five-exit line stood together | As the five exits, `Dropped{Rejected}` + halt after error · the steer error line is for steer only | We separated the scope of the two lines by source reading (the behavior is edition 8's five exits as they are) | orchestrator (source reading) |
| claude receipt-possible = the first `command_lifecycle` line (§5-4 · §7-1) | Only a **line it can recognize** (a non-empty `command_uuid` + a known status word) | With vendor drift the row would never close — an unknown line stays in `Unknown` → an init without the capability → `Unavailable` | review (P3) |
| claude line-buffer overflow `Error` is also a turn error (§5-0 · §7-1) | Only the `Error` of a failed `result` (prefix `RESULT_FAILURE_DETAIL`) | An out-of-turn overflow would fold the next clean turn as an error and an unbounded halt would stand | review (P1) |
| `last_end_failed` = the same lock · the same seq rule (§5-0) | The halt field's own cursor (`last_end_seq` · `error_seq`) | If filtered by the progress cursor, progress written first drops `Failed` and an error turn folds as clean | review (P1) |
| Termination synthesis also 「비었다」 · rings the doorbell at once (§5-0 writing order ②) | `finish` neither writes nor rings and `forget` the table field · held mail goes to the next incarnation | There is no reason to write to or ring for a field about to be erased | review (P1) |
| The registry is owned by the session · the core is an observer (§5-3) | The core holds `Arc` (`with_queued(QueuedWiring)`) · the session goes through the core | By construction there is one `Arc` | implementation |
| Three lock orders `input_order → replay → queued_inputs` (§5-0 · §5-3 · §8) | Four (+ the waiting list table leaf) · codex `Announcer.order → 상태` · two fan-out exceptions (`input_order` · `order`) | 「찼다」 is written inside the replay lock · announce emits while holding `order` | implementation |
| Re-attach: an id not in the snapshot = the accumulator state as it is (§5-7) | If an open row is not in the snapshot and there is no `Queued` of that id after S, a **revival-possible tombstone** (no bubble) | The premise is false after the ring head jumps — a ghost row | review (P5) |
| The hold is bounded by the view buffer ceiling (4 MiB · 8192) (§5-7) | A separate hold ceiling **2048 frames · 1 MiB** | Smaller than the ring ceiling (4096 · 2 MiB) | review (P5) |
| When the replay request goes — no statement | It waits for the output Channel registration (`awaitOutputChannel`) | A replay before registration makes a permanent blank screen under the hold rule | review (P5 F1) |
| codex `Delivered` twice per item (§5-2) | Once (the notification that comes first · twice only when the item id exceeds the ceiling) | The decoder filters the second by item id | implementation |
| `gen` = a number raised for each new active turn id (§5-5) | The turn mark `TurnState::Active.seq` | The same value — no counter is needed | implementation |
| Boundary after a late receipt — no statement | A display-only boundary after a receipt that came at Idle (`TurnEnd{None, Completed}` · a door with no observation) | 「Wait」 would never turn off · through the usual door it would erase the halt after error | orchestrator (P1b · P2c decision) |
| Bus error `[NOT_FOUND]` (§5-6 · §7-1) | Both verbs `[NOT_FOUND, CONFLICT]` · bus lease rejection = `CONFLICT` · WS lease rejection = a bare wording | Ambiguous target · reuse of the lease rejection code | orchestrator |
| A field to carry the turn that used the debt to the end was rejected (§5-5 「내는 순간 푼다」) | The release is still at the moment of issue · `debt_paid_by` stands **for warning only** | To emit the warning + counting (`unpaid`) we have to know that turn | implementation |
| If M15 is red, stop and ask (§9 P7 · §0 · §3-3) | Plan (plan §6 §4.9) = if red, leave it as `Immediate` with no P8 and give the final report — **it did not arise because M15 was green and P8 landed** (operating default = `AtEarliestBoundary` · including the answer segment) | The ground cited = the design stance (§10-1 22) · what is lost is only holding. ★It diverged from PRD R8 · PRD §10 · ADR-0231 decision 2 「코드 전에 사용자에게」 and was outside the proviso of §10-1 23★ | orchestrator (plan — diverged from PRD R8 · did not arise) |
| When a held answer segment is released by an other-class item — no statement (segment table 「그 밖 = 곧바로」) | The reader wakes the writer (it does not set a boundary signal) | If it did not, it would stay until the next sweep and could miss the check of waiting items · tracking label `hop="zone"` (P8-fix) | implementation (P8) |
| `SpawnedSession` field (§5-0 · §9) | `SpawnParts` field | That type does not exist | orchestrator (plan) |
| 「입력 영향」 marker shape = a P4 interface (§5-6) | The list constant next to the declaration `INPUT_AFFECTING` | It does not shake the command crate macro | orchestrator (plan) |
| What the shared input ceiling `INPUT_QUEUE_LIMIT` counts — no statement (§5-5) | 32 = all of pending (including acceptance unknown · Direct · mail) | They are visible in the list and can be removed with ✕ | orchestrator (plan) |
| Resume restore = `queued_command` lines (§5-4) | Only `commandMode: "prompt"` | We did not read other modes in source | implementation |
